- **app-runner**: Use to start, stop, restart, or check the status of the ticketing platform's full local stack (infra + all 5 backend services + gateway + frontend) — the operational counterpart to docker-infra, which owns the compose/Dockerfile files themselves. Use whenever asked to run/launch/start/stop the app, or to verify a change works end to end against a live stack. Do not use to change docker-compose.yml, a Dockerfile, or any application code — this agent only orchestrates already-existing run commands. ([ArifTarp/ticketing-platform](https://github.com/ArifTarp/ticketing-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/app-runner.md))

- **ar-1**: Use to run an AR-1 (Design Challenge) review per a project's Adversarial Review Protocol. Fires during Phase 0, after the README (0.1) and step 0.2's twin ask, and before types.ts locks the contract. Provide the README updates and every twin the recorded value names — a value may name more than one (at twin-doc none there is none, the recorded answer discharges 0.2, and the README is the complete input) — plus design notes and pointers to existing codebase patterns. ([codeschoolinabox/spiralearn](https://github.com/codeschoolinabox/spiralearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ar-1.md) · ★2)
- **ar-2**: Use to run an AR-2 (Architectural Sketch Challenge) review per a project's Adversarial Review Protocol. Fires after the architectural sketch is written in DOCS.md (inside Phase 0 step 0.3), before the review-and-commit that closes Phase 0. Provide the DOCS.md sketch, README.md, and types.ts. ([codeschoolinabox/spiralearn](https://github.com/codeschoolinabox/spiralearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ar-2.md) · ★2)
- **ar-3**: Use to run an AR-3 (Test Strategy Challenge) review per a project's Adversarial Review Protocol. Fires after the first failing test is written for an increment, before implementation. Provide the test file, the stub/types being tested, related existing tests, and the peer DOCS.md data flow diagram. ([codeschoolinabox/spiralearn](https://github.com/codeschoolinabox/spiralearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ar-3.md) · ★2)
- **ar-4**: Use to run an AR-4 (Implementation Audit) review per a project's Adversarial Review Protocol. Fires after self-review for an increment, before merge. Provide the implementation file(s), the test file, types, the DOCS.md architectural sketch (including Mermaid data flow diagram), and any utilities used. ([codeschoolinabox/spiralearn](https://github.com/codeschoolinabox/spiralearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ar-4.md) · ★2)
- **ar-5**: Use to run an AR-5 (Pre-Merge Review) review per a project's Adversarial Review Protocol. Fires after all increments complete, before the commit prompt. Provide the baseline SHA (recorded at plan approval), the modified file paths, the original task description, and the Phase 0 spec paths for modified modules — README.md, types.ts, DOCS.md, and every twin document the recorded value names, which may be more than one (at twin-doc none there is none; the commit body's settings line is what discharges step 0.2). The reviewer pulls its own diff. ([codeschoolinabox/spiralearn](https://github.com/codeschoolinabox/spiralearn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ar-5.md) · ★2)

- **ar-coder**: The coder of the inner loop.
